A[(size_t)gi*N + gk] : 0.0;\n        }\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < (BN*BK)/(THREADS_X*THREADS_Y); ++r) {\n            const int idx = tid + r * (THREADS_X*THREADS_Y);\n            const int kk = idx / BN;\n            const int nn = idx % BN;\n            const int gk = k0 + kk;\n            const int gj = blockCol + nn;\n            Bs[kk][nn] = (gk < N && gj < N) ? B[(size_t)gk*N + gj] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < BK; ++kk) {\n            double a[TM];\n            double b[TN];\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) a[i] = As[kk][threadIdx.y + i*THREADS_Y];\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) b[j] = Bs[kk][threadIdx.x + j*THREADS_X];\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n                #pragma unroll\n                for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n                    acc[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    #pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n        const int gi = blockRow + threadIdx.y + i*THREADS_Y;\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n            const int gj = blockCol + threadIdx.x + j*THREADS_X;\n            if (gi < N && gj < N) C[(size_t)gi*N + gj] = acc[i][j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n}\n```\n\nWait: `__syncthreads()` after compute before next load is needed to avoid overwriting shared while others still reading. Each block is\n// processed by a 16 x 16 thread block; every thread owns a TM x TN register\n// tile.  The K dimension is streamed through shared memory in BK-sized slabs.\n// Because K is always traversed in increasing order, every output element is\n// accumulated in exactly the same order as the reference implementation.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int BM = 64;          // rows of C per block\nconstexpr int BN = 64;          // columns of C per block\nconstexpr int BK = 16;          // K elements per shared-memory slab\nconstexpr int TM = 4;           // rows of C per thread\nconstexpr int TN = 4;           // columns of C per thread\nconstexpr int THREADS_X = 16;   // threads along N\nconstexpr int THREADS_Y = 16;   // threads along M\nconstexpr int THREADS = THREADS_X * THREADS_Y;\n\nconstexpr int A_LOADS = (BM * BK) / THREADS;\nconstexpr int B_LOADS = (BN * BK) / THREADS;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)\nvoid matmulKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,\n                  const double* __restrict__ B,\n                  double* __restrict__ C,\n                  const int N) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.y * THREADS_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    double acc[TM][TN] = {};\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n        // Stage A and B slabs into shared memory (coalesced global reads).\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < A_LOADS; ++r) {\n            const int idx = tid + r * THREADS;\n            const int kk = idx / BM;\n            const int mm = idx % BM;\n            const int gi = blockRow + mm;\n            const int gk = k0 + kk;\n            As[kk][mm] = (gi < N && gk < N) ? 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n                    acc[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n {\n    if (N == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t bytes = N * N * sizeof(double);\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dC = nullptr;\n\n Each block is\n// processed by a 16 x 16 thread block; every thread owns a TM x TN register\n// tile.  The K dimension is streamed through shared memory in BK-sized slabs.\n// Because K is always traversed in increasing order, every output element is\n// accumulated in exactly the same order as the reference implementation.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int BM = 64;          // rows of C per block\nconstexpr int BN = 64;          // columns of C per block\nconstexpr int BK = 16;          // K elements per shared-memory slab\nconstexpr int TM = 4;           // rows of C per thread\nconstexpr int TN = 4;           // columns of C per thread\nconstexpr int THREADS_X = 16;   // threads along N\nconstexpr int THREADS_Y = 16;   // threads along M\nconstexpr int THREADS = THREADS_X * THREADS_Y;\n\nconstexpr int A_LOADS = (BM * BK) / THREADS;\nconstexpr int B_LOADS = (BN * BK) / THREADS;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)\nvoid matmulKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,\n                  const double* __restrict__ B,\n                  double* __restrict__ C,\n                  const int N) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.y * THREADS_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    double acc[TM][TN] = {};\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n        // Stage A and B slabs into shared memory (coalesced global reads).\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < A_LOADS; ++r) {\n            const int idx = tid + r * THREADS;\n            const int kk = idx / BM;\n            const int mm = idx % BM;\n            const int gi = blockRow + mm;\n            const int gk = k0 + kk;\n            As[kk][mm] = (gi < N && gk < N) ?
